README.md:A personal dashboard built with Next.js and Supabase. Aggregates tools you use daily into a single customizable layout.
README.md:supabase start
README.md:To stop: `supabase stop`(preserves data) or`supabase stop --no-backup` (wipes data).
README.md:supabase db pull # sync remote schema → local migrations
README.md:supabase db push # apply local migrations → remote
README.md:supabase gen types typescript --local > types/supabase.ts

docs/development/CURRENT_SPRINT.md:- [ ] Layout plan generator: intent output → ordered widget config
docs/development/KNOWN_ISSUES.md:- No compact heatmap option for widgets
docs/development/KNOWN_ISSUES.md:- No option to edit world clock times to different countries
docs/development/KNOWN_ISSUES.md:- Weather widget performs three potentially slow steps on a cold cache: geolocation permission/lookup, Open-Meteo fetch, and BigDataCloud reverse geocode. `features/weather/hooks/useWeather.ts` also waits up to 10 seconds for geolocation before resolving to a denied/error state.
docs/development/KNOWN_ISSUES.md:- Recovery tracker ordering does an `order.includes` lookup for every tracker in `features/recovery/components/TrackerGrid.tsx`. This is fine for a few cards but becomes O(n²) as tracker count grows.
docs/development/KNOWN_ISSUES.md:- `features/recovery/components/TrackerGrid.tsx` parses localStorage as `string[]` without schema validation. Invalid shapes can survive in state and produce odd ordering behavior.
docs/development/KNOWN_ISSUES.md:- `features/settings/components/AppearancePage.tsx` applies dark mode only inside that page. Other pages may flash or ignore the saved theme until this page is visited unless a global provider applies the class.
docs/development/KNOWN_ISSUES.md:- `features/chess/analysis/hooks/useAnalysisBoard.ts` calls `setState` during render to sync controlled PGN changes. This can cause cascading renders and is risky with React Compiler/strict rendering.

docs/blueprint/ARCHITECTURE.md:- Tokens stored in Supabase session; refreshed via `@supabase/ssr`
docs/n8n-integration.md:- n8n owns scheduled syncs, external webhooks, async ingestion, notification pipelines, enrichment workflows, and long-running automation.
docs/n8n-integration.md:- `app/api/workflows/trigger/route.ts` supports explicit user-triggered actions and returns `202`.
docs/n8n-integration.md:Dashboard resolvers should read stored data produced by workflows. They should not trigger n8n during normal page load.
docs/n8n-integration.md:- n8n API keys stay server-only.
docs/widget-data-orchestration.md:Feature-specific domain logic should live under `features/(feature)/server/` when it is server-only orchestration:

.env.example:# sentry.io → Your Project → Settings → Client Keys (DSN)
docs/agents/AGENTS.md:- **Hook count** — ≤6 `use[A-Z]_`calls per`.tsx`file, counted across the **entire file** (all components combined). Files in`/hooks/`are exempt.
docs/agents/AGENTS.md:**Reducing hook count in`.tsx`files:**
docs/agents/AGENTS.md:Move hook calls to a`.ts`custom hook in`/hooks/`. Sub-components in the same `.tsx`file all count toward the file total. If a file has multiple components each calling 2–3 hooks, extract them all into one custom hook.

docs/development/COMMANDS.md:Config lives at `~/.config/starship.toml`. Initialized via `eval "$(starship init zsh)"` in `.zshrc` (already set).
docs/development/COMMANDS.md:starship config                    # Open starship.toml in $EDITOR
docs/development/COMMANDS.md:rg "import.*supabase" features/    # Search only within a specific subdirectory
docs/development/COMMANDS.md:supabase start                     # Start the full local Supabase stack (Postgres, Auth, Storage, etc.)
docs/development/COMMANDS.md:supabase stop                      # Stop the local stack (preserves data)
docs/development/COMMANDS.md:supabase stop --no-backup          # Stop and wipe all local data
docs/development/COMMANDS.md:supabase db pull                   # Sync remote schema changes down to local migration files
docs/development/COMMANDS.md:supabase db push                   # Apply local migrations to the remote database
docs/development/COMMANDS.md:supabase db psql                   # Open a psql shell to the local database
docs/development/COMMANDS.md:supabase gen types typescript --local > types/supabase.ts   # Regenerate TS types from local schema
docs/development/COMMANDS.md:supabase migration new <name>      # Create a new blank migration file
